    WHERE NOT (b."IsDeleted")
) AS b0 ON o."BrandId" = b0."Id"
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT c."Id", c."CustomerName"
    FROM aquora_tenant_code_company."Customers" AS c
    WHERE NOT (c."IsDeleted")
) AS c0 ON o."DistributorId" = c0."Id"
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT o0."Id", o0."VehicleNumber"
    FROM aquora_tenant_code_company."OperationsVisits" AS o0
    WHERE NOT (o0."IsDeleted")
) AS o1 ON o."VisitId" = o1."Id"

ORDER BY CASE
    WHEN o."Priority" = 'Immediate' THEN 3
    WHEN o."Priority" = 'High' THEN 2

END DESC, o."CreatedAt"

    WHERE NOT (c."IsDeleted")
) AS c0 ON o."DistributorId" = c0."Id"

    SELECT o0."Id", o0."VehicleNumber"

) AS o1 ON o."VisitId" = o1."Id"

    WHEN o."Priority" = 'High' THEN 2
